The ten Paper I candidates listed at review01 have completed their scoped comparison or contextual disposition. Prior exposure to common inputs and the after-reading cusp-constant calculation are explicit.

Structural work has consolidated the canonical ring, proper period family, full automorphism group, root/cusp foundations and tangent/deformation theorem. ## Corrected proof scope: acyclic normalization conductors — 2026-11-22T07:44:35.928644+00:00

In Paper II, Proposition `night:P4.35` (tag02X8), original proof items1 and3 assumed a connected conductor tree, while the general assertion also permits a disconnected forest. The connected-curve formula was correct on its stated domain; the missing case required the component count k=b0(D). The repaired formula is beta=k−c+sum_q(r_q−1). The unchanged branch counts r_plus>=n and r_minus>=c make beta>=k>=1, contradicting the forest hypothesis in the full stated domain. The diagnostic D=P1 disjoint union P1 has k=c=2 and true graph rank0; applying the connected formula there gives−1 and demonstrates the scope omission. The proposition and all conclusions remain unchanged. Both original displays and all eight proof items are retained. The original rational-exceptional-fibre cohomological route is fully checked using Grauert section6 Hauptsatze I/IIa,p59, the complete1963 corrigendum, finite-thickening cohomology and coherence/completion/Nakayama. The primary comparison occurred after release, as recorded.
